# AGENTS.md

Instructions for autonomous and semi-autonomous agents working in ACMS.

## Authority model

Humans set product intent, approve scope, approve significant decisions, define mandatory governance boundaries, and approve merges.

Agents may inspect the repository, plan, implement approved requirements, decompose approved scope into tasks, create tests, update affected documentation, create handoffs, add out-of-scope discoveries to `FUTURE_WORK.md`, draft ADRs/TDRs, and execute non-destructive work inside explicitly approved scope/budget/security/environment boundaries.

Agents must not silently add/remove product scope, promote future work, accept their own new significant ADR, merge their own feature work, exceed a budget, cross tenant/context boundaries, deploy to production, alter credentials/permissions, perform destructive infrastructure actions, or modify protected Organization/Product/Project/Feature context without required human authorization.

## Read before planning

For every meaningful change read:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `SPRINT.md`
4. `docs/BUSINESS_PROBLEMS.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. relevant accepted/proposed ADRs
7. `FUTURE_WORK.md` when scope/follow-up work is involved
8. `INITIAL_IDEAS.md` when source provenance matters

## Requirement discipline

Implementation must trace to one or more `ACMS-REQ-###` entries. Before editing summarize the requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, files/components likely to change, assumptions, and validation.

If work is outside approved requirements, ask for human approval or record it in `FUTURE_WORK.md`.

## Future-work provenance

Every future-work item must identify one of:

- **Human-Directed**
- **Agent-Discovered**
- **Source-Derived**

Human-Directed does not mean approved; it remains future scope until promoted.

## Centralized governance, decentralized execution

ACMS controls approved assignment, scope, budget, context access, permissions, and governance. It must not become the synchronous permission service for every shell command/tool call/test/retry.

A worker agent may autonomously execute implementation details inside its approved envelope.

An agent may have:

- one primary ACMS work assignment; and
- zero or more registered background routines/cron jobs.

## Context authority

Protected context levels:

- Organization
- Product
- Project
- Feature

Agents may propose changes but may not modify these without human authorization or explicit delegated authority.

Agent, Task, and Live Session context may be modified within approved policy.

If approved scope appears wrong, execute it as far as reasonably possible, preserve state, create a handoff, and propose the alternate direction instead of silently changing scope.

## Handoffs

Meaningful work must produce/update a Markdown handoff recording:

1. assigned goal and requirement IDs;
2. what was attempted;
3. what actually changed;
4. what the agent believes the current state is;
5. validation and results;
6. blockers/assumptions/unresolved questions;
7. recommended next action.

## ADR policy

Human-explicit durable architecture decisions may be recorded as `Accepted`. Agent-originated significant decisions remain `Proposed` until human approval.

## Approval timeout policy

- Reversible in-scope decision: checkpoint/preserve state, use documented fallback, continue if safe.
- Consequential but deferable action: defer that action and continue unrelated work.
- Mandatory human gate: never infer approval from timeout; hold/deny the gated action and continue only unrelated authorized work.

## Security

- Never commit credentials/secrets/customer-sensitive data.
- External/customer agents are a separate trust domain.
- Internal Agent Bridge endpoints are private-network/VPN endpoints unless an accepted ADR says otherwise.
- Do not weaken authentication/authorization/audit to simplify implementation.

## Git workflow

The documentation bootstrap is human-authorized for direct import. After bootstrap, feature work must use a dedicated branch and PR.

Recommended branch: `type/ACMS-short-description`.

Recommended commit: `type(scope): short description (ACMS-REQ-###)` plus issue/ticket when available.

Every feature PR must list requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, validation, risks, ADR/TDR changes, future work, and reviewer focus. Agents do not self-merge.

{"content": "1|\"\"\"ONE durable Jira reconciliation service (window-5 plan §13.4–§13.6).\n2|\n3|Every trigger — manual \"Check Jira now\", the durable scheduled poll, bootstrap\n4|start requests, (future) webhooks — calls :func:`reconcile` through\n5|:func:`start_reconciliation_run`, which:\n6|\n7|1. coalesces overlapping requests via a durable lease (one live run; duplicate\n8|   clicks return the EXISTING run id — idempotent),\n9|2. fetches ALL paginated ready/AI-assigned candidate issues AND re-fetches\n10|   every linked nonterminal issue by stable id regardless of current\n11|   assignment/status (a ready-only search cannot detect withdrawal),\n12|3. records append-only observations (partial scans marked partial — never\n13|   advanced as success),\n14|4. computes desired state per §13.2 (mapping table, precedence, generations),\n15|5. applies pause/stop BEFORE new starts for the same goal,\n16|6. emits durable audit events; idsempotent dispatch via generation keys.\n17|\n18|State-mapping core (§13.2) — observed Jira state drives ACMS:\n19|\n20|| Observation                                   | ACMS action                       |\n21||-----------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------|\n22|| Ready + AI assignee, no prior start            | import/queue/start (gen 1)       |\n23|| Ready + AI assignee, already queued/running    | reconcile, no duplicate          |\n24|| In Progress + AI assignee + proven gen         | continue/reconcile               |\n25|| In Progress + AI assignee, no proven ready     | Attention; never infer auth      |\n26|| On Hold/Paused/Blocked                         | reversible pause fan-out         |\n27|| Backlog/non-ready after kickoff                | pause; unstarted never start     |\n28|| Reassigned away / unassigned                   | pause immediately; block dispatch|\n29|| In Review                                      | stop launching; checkpoint       |\n30|| Canceled/Won't Do                              | stop generation + descendants    |\n31|| Done/Closed                                    | stop remaining; no fake evidence |\n32|| Unknown/unavailable/read failure               | unknown; inhibit; Attention      |\n33|\n34|Precedence: explicit terminal/cancel > local stop/pause > assignment mismatch\n35|> unknown > non-ready. content.append({\n252|                    \"type\": \"codeBlock\",\n253|                    \"attrs\": {\"language\": \"text\"},\n254|                    \"content\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": {"content": "1|\"\"\"Work-scoped Jira UI actions (window-5 plan §13.5).\n2|\n3|- \"Check Jira now\" — real authenticated POST mutation (never a GET); shows the\n4|  durable run id + per-outcome counts; duplicate clicks coalesce server-side.\n5|- Persistent Pause/Stop controls on work detail (local holds survive polls).\n6|- Jira linkage panel on work detail (issue key/url, last observed status,\n7|  assignee, eligibility verdict + reason, last checked).\n8|\"\"\"\n9|from __future__ import annotations\n10|\n11|from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\n12|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n13|\n14|from ..db import get_session\n15|from ..jira_api import (HoldRequest, LinkRequest, ReconcileRequest,\n16|                        check_jira_now, clear_hold, create_hold, link_issue)\n17|from ..jira_gate import gate_work_item\n18|from ..jira_reconcile import due_for_scheduled_poll\n19|from ..work_models import WorkItemRecord\n20|from .roles import Role\n21|from .session_auth import current_user\n22|from .work_routes import RedirectSeeOther, _require_admin\n23|\n24|router = APIRouter(prefix=\"/ui/jira\", include_in_schema=False)\n25|\n26|\n27|@router.post(\"/check-now\")\n28|async def ui_check_jira_now(\n29|    request: Request,\n30|    user=Depends(current_user),\n31|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n32|):\n33|    \"\"\"Check Jira now (execution-capable action — stated in the UI).\n34|\n35|    Runs the ONE reconciliation service immediately: imports/starts eligible\n36|    work, requests pauses/stops per current Jira state. Returns to the origin\n37|    with the durable run id in the redirect.\n38|    \"\"\"\n39|    _require_admin(user)\n40|    body = ReconcileRequest(actor=f\"ui:{user.username}\")\n41|    outcome = await check_jira_now(body, db)\n42|    return RedirectSeeOther(\n43|        f\"/ui/work?jira_run={outcome.run_id}&jira_state={outcome.state}\"\n44|        + (\"&jira_coalesced=1\" if outcome.coalesced else \"\")\n45|    )\n46|\n47|\n48|@router.post(\"/work/{work_item_id}/link\")\n49|async def ui_link_jira(\n50|    work_item_id: str,\n51|    request: Request,\n52|    issue_key: str = Form(..., min_length=3, max_length=64),\n53|    user=Depends(current_user),\n54|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n55|):\n56|    _require_admin(user)\n57|    body = LinkRequest(issue_key=issue_key.strip(), actor=f\"ui:{user.username}\")\n58|    try:\n59|        result = await link_issue(work_item_id, body, db)\n60|        err = None\n61|    except Exception as e:  # human-readable failure per plan §14\n62|        result = None\n63|        err = str(getattr(e, \"detail\", None) or e)[:300]\n64|    target = f\"/ui/work/{work_item_id}\"\n65|    if err:\n66|        from .work_routes import RedirectWithError\n67|\n68|        return RedirectWithError(target, f\"Jira link failed: {err}\")\n69|    gate = result[\"gate\"]\n70|    return RedirectSeeOther(f\"{target}?jira_gate={gate['verdict']}\")\n71|\n72|\n73|@router.post(\"/work/{work_item_id}/hold\")\n74|async def ui_set_hold(\n75|    work_item_id: str,\n76|    request: Request,\n77|    hold_kind: str = Form(..., pattern=\"^(PAUSE|STOP)$\"),\n78|    reason: str = Form(\"\", max_length=512),\n79|    user=Depends(current_user),\n80|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n81|):\n82|    _require_admin(user)\n83|    body = HoldRequest(hold_kind=hold_kind, reason=reason, actor=f\"ui:{user.username}\")\n84|    await create_hold(work_item_id, body, db)\n85|    return RedirectSeeOther(f\"/ui/work/{work_item_id}?hold_set={hold_kind}\")\n86|\n87|\n88|@router.post(\"/work/{work_item_id}/holds/{hold_id}/clear\")\n89|async def ui_clear_hold(\n90|    work_item_id: str,\n91|    hold_id: str,\n92|    request: Request,\n93|    user=Depends(current_user),\n94|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n95|):\n96|    _require_admin(user)\n97|    await clear_hold(work_item_id, hold_id, db)\n98|    return RedirectSeeOther(f\"/ui/work/{work_item_id}?hold_cleared=1\")\n99|\n100|\n101|@router.get(\"/status\")\n102|async def ui_jira_status(\n103|    request: Request,\n104|    user=Depends(current_user),\n105|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n106|):\n107|    \"\"\"Small JSON for the dashboard widget: scheduler freshness + gate summary.\"\"\"\n108|    due, info = await due_for_scheduled_poll(db)\n109|    return {\"due_now\": due, **info}", "total_lines": 108, "file_size": 3876, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

AsyncSession):\n562|    from .jira_models import JiraSyncSchedulerStateRecord\n563|\n564|    row = (await db.scalars(select(JiraSyncSchedulerStateRecord))).first()\n565|    if row is None:\n566|        row = JiraSyncSchedulerStateRecord(id=1)\n567|        db.add(row)\n568|    return row\n569|\n570|\n571|async def due_for_scheduled_poll(db: AsyncSession) -> tuple[bool, dict[str, Any]]:\n572|    \"\"\"Is the durable poll due? (§13.6: interval ≤ 24h enforced; UTC persisted.)\n573|\n574|    Manual polls never postpone the next scheduled run beyond the interval:\n575|    due-ness is computed from the last SUCCESSFUL poll only.\n576|    \"\"\"\n577|    s_rows = await _scheduler_state(db)\n578|    sched = s_rows\n579|    interval_h = min(max(int(getattr(get_settings(), \"jira_reconcile_interval_hours\", 24) or 24), 1), 24)\n580|    last_ok = sched.last_successful_poll_at\n581|    last_attempt = sched.last_attempt_at\n582|    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n583|    if last_ok is None:\n584|        return True, {\"reason\": \"never_run\", \"interval_hours\": interval_h,\n585|                      \"last_successful_poll_at\": None, \"last_attempt_at\": _iso(last_attempt)}\n586|    next_due = last_ok + timedelta(hours=interval_h)\n587|    return now >= next_due, {\n588|        \"reason\": \"due\" if now >= next_due else \"not_due\",\n589|        \"interval_hours\": interval_h,\n590|        \"last_successful_poll_at\": _iso(last_ok),\n591|        \"next_due_at\": _iso(next_due),\n592|        \"last_attempt_at\": _iso(last_attempt),\n593|        \"overdue\": now > next_due + timedelta(hours=interval_h),\n594|    }\n595|\n596|\n597|def _iso(dt: datetime | None) -> str | None:\n598|    return dt.isoformat() if dt else None", "total_lines": 597, "file_size": 28111, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

### [2026-10-02T05:23:00.447622+00:00] tool · tool: read_file

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Jira kickoff eligibility gate (window-5 plan §7/§13).\n2|\n3|The human-facing planning layer is Jira. A Work Item may be dispatched to AI\n4|execution ONLY when:\n5|\n6|  1. it carries a Jira linkage (site + immutable issue id + key), and\n7|  2. the linked issue's CURRENT observed status is a configured READY state\n8|     (default ``TO START`` — project workflow metadata is verified at runtime,\n9|     never assumed globally valid), and\n10|  3. the issue is CURRENTLY assigned to the dedicated AI account\n11|     (startupteamscompany@gmail.com — resolved to a Jira accountId, matched by\n12|     accountId, never by display name or email visibility), and\n13|  4. no local hold (pause/stop) or operator restart authorization is pending.\n14|\n15|Eligibility is NECESSARY but never sufficient: budgets, runtime health, and\n16|local holds still apply after this gate (dispatch order: Jira → budget → …).\n17|\n18|Server-side enforcement: dispatch_service calls :func:`gate_work_item` on every\n19|path (UI, API, scheduler, retry, resume, Executive decomposition, product\n20|bootstrap). Drafts may be saved unlinked; they are visibly non-executable.\n21|\n22|Design notes:\n23|- The gate NEVER mutates Jira. Observation is read-only; transitions are the\n24|  reconciliation service's concern.\n25|- Local holds are durable DB rows — a process restart cannot clear them and a\n26|  poll cannot override them (plan §13.3).\n27|- One Jira issue may authorize multiple ACMS Work Items (§7.3) — descendants\n28|  inherit the authorization; the gate checks the TOP-LEVEL linkage up the\n29|  parent chain as well as the item's own linkage.\n30|- Observation staleness: eligibility is verified at dispatch time with a LIVE\n31|  Jira read when credentials are configured (bounded timeout). If Jira is\n32|  unreachable the gate returns an UNKNOWN verdict — new dispatch is inhibited\n33|  (fail-closed), and running work keeps going (§13.2 last row).\n34|\"\"\"\n35|from __future__ import See\n40|  <a href=\"/ui/work?jira_history=1\">run history</a> for per-issue counts.</p>\n41|</section>\n42|{% endif %}\n43|\n44|{% if error %}\n45|<section class=\"notice warn\"><p>{{ error }}</p></section>\n46|{% endif %}\n47|\n48|<section class=\"panel\">\n49|  <h2>Filters</h2>\n50|  <form method=\"get\" action=\"/ui/work\" class=\"filter-form\">\n51|    <label>Status\n52|      <select name=\"status_filter\">\n53|        <option value=\"\">all</option>\n54|        {% for s in statuses %}\n55|        <option value=\"{{ s }}\" {% if status_filter == s %}selected{% endif %}>{{ s }}</option>\n56|        {% endfor %}\n57|      </select>\n58|    </label>\n59|    <label>Kind\n60|      <select name=\"kind_filter\">\n61|        <option value=\"\">all</option>\n62|        {% for k detail=\"Administrator role required\")\n53|\n54|\n55|def _badge(kind: str) -> str:\n56|    return f\"badge badge-{kind}\"\n57|\n58|\n59|async def _work_prs(db, work_item_id: str) -> list[dict]:\n60| {"content": "1|\"\"\"Work orchestration service layer (ACMS-REQ-007/008/013/014/036).\n2|\n3|Rules enforced here (not just at the API):\n4|- One ACTIVE primary assignment per agent (ACMS-REQ-008). Assigning new primary\n5|  work requires the previous assignment to be COMPLETED/RELEASED/SUSPENDED first.\n6|- Work item hierarchy: parent must exist; any kind may nest.\n7|- Routines are inventory-only: upsert by (agent_id, name); ACMS never schedules.\n8|- Execution tasks always trace to a work item (ACMS-REQ-036).\n9|\"\"\"\n10|from __future__ import annotations\n11|\n12|import uuid\n13|from datetime import datetime, timezone\n14|\n15|from sqlalchemy import select\n16|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n17|\n18|from .models import AgentRecord\n19|from .work_keys import allocate_assignment_key\n20|from .uid_keys import allocate_work_uid\n21|from .work_models import (\n22|    AssignmentCreate,\n23|    AssignmentRecord,\n24|    BackgroundRoutineRecord,\n25|    ExecutionTaskCreate,\n26|    ExecutionTaskRecord,\n27|    HandoffCreate,\n28|    HandoffRecord,\n29|    RoutineUpsert,\n30|    WorkItemCreate,\n31|    WorkItemRecord,\n32|    WorkItemStatus,\n33|    WorkItemUpdate,\n34|)\n35|\n36|\n37|def _now() -> datetime:\n38|    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n39|\n40|\n41|def _new_id() -> str:\n42|    return str(uuid.uuid4())\n43|\n44|\n45|# ---------------------------------------------------------------- work items\n46|\n47|\n48|async def create_work_item(db: AsyncSession, payload: WorkItemCreate) -> WorkItemRecord:\n49|    if payload.parent_id is not None:\n50|        parent = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, payload.parent_id)\n51|        if parent is None:\n52|            raise ValueError(f\"parent work item {payload.parent_id} does not exist\")\n53|    if payload.project_id is not None:\n54|        from .a2a_models import ProjectRecord\n55|\n56|        project = await db.get(ProjectRecord, payload.project_id)\n57|        if project is None:\n58|            raise ValueError(f\"project {payload.project_id} does not exist\")\n59|    now = _now()\n60|    seq, work_uid = await allocate_work_uid(db, now)\n61|    record = WorkItemRecord(\n62|        work_item_id=_new_id(),\n63|        work_key=f\"ACMS-WORK-{seq:06d}\",\n64|        work_uid=work_uid,\n65|        work_sequence=seq,\n66|        parent_id=payload.parent_id,\n67|        kind=payload.kind.value,\n68|        title=payload.title,\n69|        created_by=payload.created_by,\n70|        project_id=payload.project_id,\n71|        status=WorkItemStatus.PLANNED.value,\n72|        scope_markdown=payload.scope_markdown,\n73|        created_at=now,\n74|        updated_at=now,\n75|    )\n76|    db.add(record)\n77|    await db.commit()\n78|    await db.refresh(record)\n79|    return record\n80|\n81|\n82|async def get_work_item(db: AsyncSession, work_item_id: str) -> WorkItemRecord | None:\n83|    return await db.get(WorkItemRecord, work_item_id)\n84|\n85|\n86|async def list_work_items(db: AsyncSession) -> list[WorkItemRecord]:\n87|    result = await db.scalars(select(WorkItemRecord).order_by(WorkItemRecord.created_at))\n88|    return list(result.all())\n89|\n90|\n91|async def update_work_item(\n92|    db: AsyncSession, work_item_id: str, payload: WorkItemUpdate\n93|) -> WorkItemRecord | None:\n94|    record = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, work_item_id)\n95|    if record is None:\n96|        return None\n97|    if payload.status is not None:\n98|        record.status = payload.status.value\n99|    if payload.title is not None:\n100|        record.title = payload.title\n101|    if payload.scope_markdown is not None:\n102|        record.scope_markdown = payload.scope_markdown\n103|    if payload.project_id is not None:\n104|        from .a2a_models import ProjectRecord\n105|\n106|        project = await db.get(ProjectRecord, payload.project_id)\n107|        if project is None:\n108|            raise ValueError(f\"project {payload.project_id} does not exist\")\n109|        record.project_id = payload.project_id\n110|    record.updated_at = _now()\n111|    await db.commit()\n112|    await db.refresh(record)\n113|    return record\n114|\n115|\n116|# ---------------------------------------------------------------- assignments\n117|\n118|\n119|async def assign_primary(\n120|    db: AsyncSession, payload: AssignmentCreate, assigned_by: str | None = None\n121|) -> tuple[AssignmentRecord | None, str | None]:\n122|    \"\"\"Create a primary assignment, enforcing the ACMS-REQ-008 invariant.\n123|\n124|    Returns (record, None) on success, or (None, error_code) where error_code is\n125|    one of: 'unknown-agent' | 'unknown-work-item' | 'work-item-not-open' |\n126|    'agent-has-active-assignment'.\n127|    \"\"\"\n128|    agent = await db.get(AgentRecord, payload.agent_id)\n129|    if agent is None:\n130|        return None, \"unknown-agent\"\n131|    work_item = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, payload.work_item_id)\n132|    if work_item is None:\n133|        return None, \"unknown-work-item\"\n134|    if work_item.status not in (WorkItemStatus.PLANNED.value, WorkItemStatus.ACTIVE.value):\n135|        return None, \"work-item-not-open\"\n136|\n137|    open_assignment = (\n138|        await db.scalars(\n139|            select(AssignmentRecord).where(\n140|                AssignmentRecord.agent_id == payload.agent_id,\n141|                AssignmentRecord.status == \"ACTIVE\",\n142|            )\n143|        )\n144|    ).first()\n145|    if open_assignment is not None:\n146|        return None, \"agent-has-active-assignment\"\n147|\n148|    record = AssignmentRecord(\n149|        assignment_id=_new_id(),\n150|        assignment_key=await allocate_assignment_key(db),\n151|        agent_id=payload.agent_id,\n152|        work_item_id=payload.work_item_id,\n153|        status=\"ACTIVE\",\n154|        assigned_by=assigned_by,\n155|        assigned_at=_now(),\n156|        closed_at=None,\n157|    )\n158|    db.add(record)\n159|    # Activating an assignment activates the work item (PLANNED -> ACTIVE).\n160|    if work_item.status == WorkItemStatus.PLANNED.value:\n161|        work_item.status = WorkItemStatus.ACTIVE.value\n162|        work_item.updated_at = _now()\n163|    await db.commit()\n164|    await db.refresh(record)\n165|    return record, None\n166|\n167|\n168|async def close_assignment(\n169|    db: AsyncSession, assignment_id: str, new_status: str\n170|) -> AssignmentRecord | None:\n171|    \"\"\"Close an assignment: COMPLETED | RELEASED | SUSPENDED.\"\"\"\n172|    if new_status not in {\"COMPLETED\", \"RELEASED\", \"SUSPENDED\"}:\n173|        raise ValueError(f\"invalid assignment close status: {new_status}\")\n174|    record = await db.get(AssignmentRecord, assignment_id)\n175|    if record is None or record.status != \"ACTIVE\":\n176|        return None\n177|    record.status = new_status\n178|    record.closed_at = _now()\n179|    await db.commit()\n180|    await db.refresh(record)\n181|    return record\n182|\n183|\n184|async def list_assignments(\n185|    db: AsyncSession,\n186|    agent_id: str | None = None,\n187|    status: str | None = None,\n188|    work_item_id: str | None = None,\n189|) -> list[AssignmentRecord]:\n190|    stmt = select(AssignmentRecord).order_by(AssignmentRecord.assigned_at)\n191|    if agent_id is not None:\n192|        stmt = stmt.where(AssignmentRecord.agent_id == agent_id)\n193|    if status is not None:\n194|        stmt = stmt.where(AssignmentRecord.status == status)\n195|    if work_item_id is not None:\n196|        stmt = stmt.where(AssignmentRecord.work_item_id == work_item_id)\n197|    result = await db.scalars(stmt)\n198|    return list(result.all())\n199|\n200|\n201|async def list_children(db: AsyncSession, parent_id: str) -> list[WorkItemRecord]:\n202|    \"\"\"Direct children of a work item (ACMS-REQ-007 hierarchy).\"\"\"\n203|    result = await db.scalars(\n204|        select(WorkItemRecord)\n205|        .where(WorkItemRecord.parent_id == parent_id)\n206|        .order_by(WorkItemRecord.created_at)\n207|    )\n208|    return list(result.all())\n209|\n210|\n211|async def active_assignment_for_agent(db: AsyncSession, agent_id: str) -> AssignmentRecord | None:\n212|    return (\n213|        await db.scalars(\n214|            select(AssignmentRecord).where(\n215|                AssignmentRecord.agent_id == agent_id,\n216|                AssignmentRecord.status == \"ACTIVE\",\n217|            )\n218|        )\n219|    ).first()\n220|\n221|", "total_lines": 360, "file_size": 12258, "truncated": true, "hint": "Use offset=221 to continue {"content": "1|\"\"\"A2A work dispatch — the single authoritative path for starting NEW cloud\n2|execution of a Work Item on a worker agent (REV2 Phase C, REQ-059 gate).\n3|\n4|Design rules from the plan:\n5|- ONE dispatch point. UI/API layers call this service; budget checks are never\n6|  duplicated in several callers.\n7|- Gate BEFORE new cloud execution: budget execution-check verdict decides.\n8|  - below hard limit  → dispatch\n9|  - soft exceeded     → dispatch + retain the warning in the event trail\n10|  - hard exceeded     → no NEW dispatch; durable event + Attention item\n11|                        (EXECUTION_REJECTED); in-flight work untouched\n12|  - hard + override   → dispatch + audited override trail already exists\n13|- Unknown cost stays UNKNOWN — never a fabricated zero (the budget engine\n14|  itself never blocks on unknown cost; the dispatch path preserves the fact).\n15|- Idempotency: a client-supplied idempotency key (or a stable hash of the\n16|  work/assignment pair) maps to ONE execution task; retries return the\n17|  original task instead of dispatching twice.\n18|- Correlation recorded on the task + event: Work ID/key, assignment, bridge\n19|  run id, budget-check verdict (audit trail), external A2A task id.\n20|\"\"\"\n21|from __future__ import annotations\n22|\n23|import hashlib\n24|import json\n25|from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n26|from typing import Any\n27|\n28|from sqlalchemy import select\n29|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n30|\n31|from . import budget_service, work_service\n32|from .session_lifecycle import fail_session_for_dispatch, open_session_for_dispatch\n33|from .bridge import BridgeError, get_bridge_for_agent\n34|from .telemetry_models import AgentEventRecord\n35|from .telemetry_service import add_event\n36|from .work_models import ExecutionTaskCreate, ExecutionTaskRecord, WorkItemRecord\n37|\n38|\n39|ASSIGNMENT_SCHEMA_VERSION = \"acms-a2a-assignment-v1\"\n40|\n41|\n42|class DispatchError(Exception):\n43|    \"\"\"Machine-readable dispatch failure (message = stable error code).\"\"\"\n44|\n45|    def __init__(self, code: str, http_status: int = 409, detail: dict | None = None):\n46|        super().__init__(code)\n47|        self.code = code\n48|        self.http_status = http_status\n49|        self.detail = detail or {}\n50|\n51|\n52|def _idempotency_key(work_item_id: str, assignment_id: str | None,\n53|                     client_key: str | None) -> str:\n54|    \"\"\"Stable dedupe key. Client key wins; else the open assignment; else work.\"\"\"\n55|    basis = client_key or assignment_id or work_item_id\n56|    return hashlib.sha256(f\"acms-dispatch:{basis}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]\n57|\n58|\n59|async def _check_worker_busy(db: AsyncSession, agent_id: str, work_item_id: str) -> dict | None:\n60|    \"\"\"One task per worker (plan §3; ADR-0013): refuse a NEW dispatch when the\n61|    target agent already has a non-terminal execution task for a DIFFERENT\n62|    work item. Returns the blocking detail or None.\"\"\"\n63|    rows = (await db.scalars(\n64|        select(ExecutionTaskRecord)\n65|        .where(ExecutionTaskRecord.agent_id == agent_id,\n66|               ExecutionTaskRecord.status == \"RUNNING\")\n67|        .order_by(ExecutionTaskRecord.started_at.desc())\n68|        .limit(10)\n69|    )).all()\n70|    for t in rows:\n71|        if t.work_item_id != work_item_id:\n72|            return {\n73|                \"blocking_work_item_id\": t.work_item_id,\n74|                \"blocking_task_id\": t.task_id,\n75|                \"blocking_since\": t.started_at.isoformat() if t.started_at else None,\n76|            }\n77|    return None\n78|\n79|\n80|def _build_assignment_envelope(\n81|    *, work: WorkItemRecord, agent_id: str, task_id: str, assignment_id: str | None,\n82|    session_id: str | None, jira_issue_key: str | None, project: dict | None,\n83|    product: dict | None, sprint: dict | None, tags: list[str],\n84|    instruction: str, completion_condition: str | None,\n85|    output_artifacts_expected: list[str], model_policy: dict, priority: str,\n86|    callback_base: str, callback_token_hint: str, idempotency_key: str,\n87|) -> dict[str, Any]:\n88|    \"\"\"acms-a2a-assignment-v1 envelope (ADR-0013; plan §12). project may never\n89|    be null; product/sprint may be.\"\"\"\n90|    return {\n91|        \"schema_version\": ASSIGNMENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n92|        \"assignment_id\": assignment_id,\n93|        \"idempotency_key\": idempotency_key,\n94|        \"agent_id\": str(agent_id),\n95|        \"work_item_id\": work.work_item_id,\n96|        \"execution_task_id\": task_id,\n97|        \"execution_session_id\": session_id,\n98|        \"work_key\": work.work_key,\n99|        \"jira_issue_key\": jira_issue_key,\n100|        \"project\": project,\n101|        \"product\": product,\n102|        \"sprint\": sprint,\n103|        \"tags\": tags,\n104|        \"instructions\": instruction,\n105|        \"completion_condition\": completion_condition,\n106|        \"output_artifacts_expected\": output_artifacts_expected,\n107|        \"model_policy\": model_policy,\n108|        \"priority\": priority,\n109|        \"callback_urls\": {\n110|            \"heartbeat\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/fleet/agents/{agent_id}/heartbeat\" if callback_base else None,\n111|            \"events\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/execution/{task_id}/stream\" if callback_base else None,\n112|            \"completion\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/callbacks/execution-completion\" if callback_base else None,\n113|            \"artifacts\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/artifacts\" if callback_base else None,\n114|            \"human_attention\": f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/inbox\" if callback_base else None,\n115|        },\n116|        \"callback_token_hint\": callback_token_hint,\n117|        \"created_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n118|    }\n119|\n120|\n121|async def _find_existing_task(db: AsyncSession, dedupe_key: str) -> Any | None:\n122|    \"\"\"The most recent task created from this dedupe key.\n123|\n124|    The dedupe ledger lives in the durable EXECUTION_DISPATCHED event metadata\n125|    (``dedupe_key`` → ``task_id``) — external_task_id now carries the A2A run\n126|    id (ADR-0012 callback binding), so it can no longer double as the idem-\n127|    potency ledger. Retry when ready.\",\n364|            item_class=\"ACTION_REQUIRED\", severity=\"medium\",\n365|            agent_id=str(target_agent), work_item_id=work_item_id,\n366|            jira_issue_key=work.jira_issue_key,\n367|            correlation_id=f\"dispatch-failed:{dedupe_key}\",\n368|            metadata={\"bridge_error\": str(e)[:300], \"actor\": actor},\n369|        )\n370|        await fail_session_for_dispatch(db, session=session_for_run, reason=str(e))\n371|        await db.commit()\n372|        raise DispatchError(\"bridge-error\", 502, {\"detail\": str(e)[:300]}) from None\n373|\n374|    run_id = (result or {}).get(\"run_id\") or (result or {}).get(\"id\")\n375|\n376|    # ACK: the run id IS the worker's acceptance receipt (ADR-0013) — the\n377|    # Hermes api-server accepted the run and returned 202 with run_id.\n378|    if run_id:\n379|        task.external_task_id = str(run_id)\n380|        task.transport_state = \"RUNNING\"\n381|\n382|    # Phase F (plan §8): the dispatch automatically owns an ExecutionSession.\n383|    # Idempotent on the A2A run id — a duplicate dispatch never duplicates it.\n384|    session_for_run = await open_session_for_dispatch(\n385|        db, agent_id=str(target_agent), work_item_id=work_item_id,\n386|        assignment_id=(active.assignment_id if active else None),\n387|        a2a_task_id=str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n388|        harness_session_id=(result or {}).get(\"session_id\"),\n389|    )\n390|\n391|    # ---- 6. execution task pre-created in step 4b (ADR-0012); the dedupe\n392|    # ledger is preserved on the event metadata (``disp-<key>`` remains the\n393|    # client-visible idempotency marker) ------------------------\n394|\n395|    # ---- 7. durable audit trail --------------------------------------------\n396|    await add_event(\n397|        db,\n398|        event_type=\"EXECUTION_DISPATCHED\",\n399|        actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n400|        agent_id=target_agent,\n401|        work_key=work.work_key,\n402|        assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n403|        a2a_task_id=str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n404|        summary=(f\"Dispatched [{work.work_key or work_item_id[:8]}] to {str(target_agent)[:8]}\"\n405|                 + (f\" (soft exceeded: {verdict.get('budget_state')})\"\n406|                    if verdict.get(\"budget_state\") == \"SOFT_EXCEEDED\" else \"\"))[:512],\n407|        metadata={\"run_id\": run_id, \"budget_check\": verdict,\n408|                  \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key, \"task_id\": task.task_id,\n409|                  \"assignment_id\": active.assignment_id if active else None,\n410|                  \"work_item_id\": work_item_id, \"actor\": actor},\n411|    )\n412|    await db.commit()\n413|\n414|    # ---- 8. runtime-driven completion watcher (ADR-0012 primary path) ------\n415|    # Infrastructure observes the bridge run and delivers the completion\n416|    # callback itself — never dependent on the model following instructions\n417|    # (window-4 plan §C1). Scheduled AFTER commit; failures never break the\n418|    # dispatch result (watch scheduling is fail-open, reconcile is fallback).\n419|    if run_id:\n420|        from .run_watcher import schedule_run_watch\n421|        from .run_event_pump import schedule_run_event_pump\n422|\n423|        schedule_run_watch(task.task_id)\n424|        # live activity visibility (ADR-0014) — fail-open, never breaks dispatch\n425|        schedule_run_event_pump(task.task_id)\n426|\n427|    return {\"dispatched\": True, \"reason\": verdict.get(\"reason\", \"dispatched\"),\n428|            \"task_id\": task.task_id, \"external_task_id\": str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n429|            \"budget_check\": verdict}\n430|", "total_lines": 429, "file_size": 20631, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"STNA-86 Jira template fidelity validation (STEA-004 plan §10).\n2|\n3|History: STNA-87 (the prior acceptance ticket) was created as a one-time\n4|approximation of STNA-86 rather than a faithful clone — a process defect this\n5|module prevents repeating.\n6|\n7|Contract for FUTURE explicitly-authorized AI-created Jira tickets:\n8|\n9|1. CLONE STNA-86 — never approximate the template from memory.\n10|2. Fetch the live STNA-86 issue fields/description as the structural source.\n11|3. Compare required sections against the candidate ticket description.\n12|4. FAIL validation when required sections are absent from the candidate.\n13|\n14|No ordinary ACMS autonomous ticket creation — validation only runs when a\n15|human explicitly authorizes a ticket clone (Jira write path stays gated).\n16|\"\"\"\n17|from __future__ import annotations\n18|\n19|import re\n20|from dataclasses import dataclass, field\n21|\n22|# Template issues that define the required structural sections.\n23|TEMPLATE_ISSUE_KEYS = (\"STNA-86\",)\n24|\n25|# Sections STNA-86 must carry (h2/h3 headings, matched case-insensitively).\n26|# Derived from the live ticket at validation time — the FROZEN list below is\n27|# the minimal acceptance-structure floor used when the live fetch is a MOCK\n28|# or unavailable (validation then reports limited-confidence).\n29|REQUIRED_SECTION_FLOOR = (\n30|    \"bluf\",\n31|    \"objective\",\n32|    \"scope\",\n33|    \"acceptance\",\n34|)\n35|\n36|\n37|@dataclass\n38|class TemplateValidationResult:\n39|    source_key: str = \"\"\n40|    source_fetch_ok: bool = False\n41|    source_sections: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n42|    candidate_sections: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n43|    missing_required: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n44|    ok: bool = False\n45|    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n46|\n47|\n48|def extract_sections(markdown_or_jira: str) -> list[str]:\n49|    \"\"\"Extract heading texts (## / ### / h2 / h3) from a Jira description.\"\"\"\n50|    sections = []\n51|    for ln in (markdown_or_jira or \"\").splitlines():\n52|        m = re.match(r\"^\\s*h[23]\\.\\s*(.+)$\", ln.strip(), re.IGNORECASE)\n53|        if m:\n54|            sections.append(m.group(1).strip().lower())\n55|            continue\n56|        m = re.match(r\"^\\s*#{2,3}\\s+(.+)$\", ln.strip())\n57|        if m:\n58|            sections.append(m.group(1).strip().lower())\n59|    return sections\n60|\n61|\n62|def _norm(text: str) -> str:\n63|    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9 ]\", \" \", text.lower()).strip()\n64|\n65|\n66|async def validate_template_fidelity(\n67|    client,\n68|    source_key: str = \"STNA-86\",\n69|    candidate_description: str | None = None,\n70|    candidate_key: str | None = None,\n71|) -> TemplateValidationResult:\n72|    \"\"\"Validate a candidate description against the live template issue.\n73|\n74|    ``client`` is a JiraClient (real or mock). Fetches the SOURCE issue\n75|    structure live, extracts its sections, and requires that every section\n76|    whose normalized name matches the acceptance floor exists in the\n77|    candidate. Additional source sections are reported as notes only.\n78|    \"\"\"\n79|    result = TemplateValidationResult(source_key=source_key)\n80|    try:\n81|        src = client.get_issue(source_key)\n82|        result.source_fetch_ok = True\n83|        result.source_sections = extract_sections(src.description or \"\")\n84|    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fetch failure = limited-confidence\n85|        result.source_fetch_ok = False\n86|        result.notes.append(f\"source fetch failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\")\n87|\n88|    effective_required = (\n89|        [s for s in result.source_sections if any(f in _norm(s) for f in REQUIRED_SECTION_FLOOR)]\n90|        or list(REQUIRED_SECTION_FLOOR)\n91|    )\n92|    if not result.source_fetch_ok:\n93|        result.notes.append(\"using REQUIRED_SECTION_FLOOR as required list (source unavailable)\")\n94|\n95|    if candidate_description is None:\n96|        if candidate_key:\n97|            try:\n98|                cand = client.get_issue(candidate_key)\n99|                candidate_description = cand.description or \"\"\n100|            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n101|                result.notes.append(f\"candidate fetch failed: {str(exc)[:120]}\")\n102|        if candidate_description is None:\n103|            result.missing_required = list(effective_required)\n104|            result.ok = False\n105|            result.notes.append(\"no candidate description provided — cannot validate\")\n106|            return result\n107|\n108|    result.candidate_sections = extract_sections(candidate_description)\n109|    cand_norm = {_norm(s) for s in result.candidate_sections}\n110|    for req in effective_required:\n111|        if not any(_norm(req) in c or c in _norm(req) for c in cand_norm):\n112|            result.missing_required.append(req)\n113|    result.ok = not result.missing_required\n114|    return result", "total_lines": 113, "file_size": 4529, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"A2A dispatch API — REV2 Phase C (budget-gated work delivery).\n2|\n3|The single machine endpoint for starting NEW cloud execution of a Work Item.\n4|Budget gating, idempotency, and correlation live in dispatch_service — this\n5|router is a thin adapter. Bearer-gated like the rest of the machine API.\n6|\"\"\"\n7|from __future__ import annotations\n8|\n9|from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request, status\n10|from pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n11|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n12|\n13|from .db import get_session\n14|from .dispatch_service import DispatchError, dispatch_work\n15|from .security import require_admin_token\n16|\n17|router = APIRouter(prefix=\"/api/v1/dispatch\", dependencies=[Depends(require_admin_token)])\n18|\n19|\n20|class DispatchRequest(BaseModel):\n21|    instruction: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=20000)\n22|    agent_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=36)\n23|    idempotency_key: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=200)\n24|    session_id: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=255)\n25|    actor: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=128)\n26|\n27|\n28|class DispatchResponse(BaseModel):\n29|    dispatched: bool\n30|    reason: str\n31|    work_item_id: str\n32|    task_id: str | None = None\n33|    external_task_id: str | None = None\n34|    budget_check: dict | None = None\n35|    worker_busy: dict | None = None\n36|    jira_gate: dict | None = None\n37|\n38|\n39|@router.post(\"/work/{work_item_id}\", response_model=DispatchResponse)\n40|async def dispatch_work_item(work_item_id: str, body: DispatchRequest,\n41|                             request: Request,\n42|                             db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)) -> DispatchResponse:\n43|    \"\"\"Dispatch NEW cloud execution for a Work Item (REQ-059 gated).\n44|\n45|    - 200 with dispatched=false + reason=hard_budget_exceeded → gate held\n46|      (durable EXECUTION_REJECTED event emitted; visible on /attention).\n47|    - 200 with dispatched=false + reason=duplicate → idempotent replay of an\n48|      in-flight dispatch (original task_id returned).\n49|    - 4xx/5xx DispatchError codes: unknown-work-item (404),\n50|      no-active-assignment (409), agent-mismatch (409), no-bridge-target (503),\n51|      bridge-error (502).\n52|    \"\"\"\n53|    actor = body.actor or getattr(request.state, \"actor\", None) or \"api\"\n54|    try:\n55|        result = await dispatch_work(\n56|            db, work_item_id=work_item_id, instruction=body.instruction,\n57|            agent_id=body.agent_id, idempotency_key=body.idempotency_key,\n58|            session_id=body.session_id, actor=actor,\n59|        )\n60|    except DispatchError as e:\n61|        raise HTTPException(status_code=e.http_status,\n62|                            detail={\"code\": e.code, **e.detail}) from None\n63|    return DispatchResponse(work_item_id=work_item_id, **result)\n64|", "total_lines": 63, "file_size": 2706, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "140|    event_id: str\n141|    sequence: int\n142|    timestamp: str\n143|    event_type: str\n144|    actor_source: str | None\n145|    agent_id: str | None\n146|    work_key: str | None\n147|    assignment_key: str | None\n148|    harness_session_id: str | None\n149|    summary: str\n150|\n151|\n152|@router.get(\"/events\", response_model=list[AgentEventView])\n153|async def get_events(\n154|    agent_id: str | None = None,\n155|    event_type: str | None = None,\n156|    limit: int = 100,\n157|    _: None = Depends(require_admin_token),\n158|    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),\n159|):\n160|    from sqlalchemy import select\n161|\n162|    stmt = select(AgentEventRecord).order_by(AgentEventRecord.sequence.desc()).limit(min(limit, 500))\n163|    if agent_id:\n164|        stmt = stmt.where(AgentEventRecord.agent_id == agent_id)\n165|    if event_type:\n166|        stmt = stmt.where(AgentEventRecord.event_type == event_type)\n167|    rows = (await db.scalars(stmt)).all()\n168|    return [\n169|        AgentEventView(\n170|            event_id=r.event_id, sequence=r.sequence, timestamp=_iso(r.timestamp),\n171|            event_type=r.event_type, actor_source=r.actor_source, agent_id=r.agent_id,\n172|            work_key=r.work_key, assignment_key=r.assignment_key,\n173|            harness_session_id=r.harness_session_id, summary=r.summary,\n174|        )\n175|        for r in rows\n176|    ]\n177|\n178|\n179|def _not_found():\n180|    from fastapi import HTTPException, status as http_status\n181|\n182|    return HTTPException(status_code=http_status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail=\"agent not found\")\n183|", "total_lines": 182, "file_size": 6348, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Jira↔ACMS sync service (v1, 2026-09-29 plan §6/Phase D).\n2|\n3|Inbound:  poll assigned Jira issues → high-level ACMS work context (one work\n4|          item per Jira issue MAX; Executive decomposes further internally).\n5|Outbound: useful lifecycle summaries posted as Jira comments (started /\n6|          milestone / blocked / PR available / completed / final handoff).\n7|NEVER:    one Jira ticket per internal Work Item or task (plan §D7 invariant).\n8|\"\"\"\n9|from __future__ import annotations\n10|\n11|from .jira_client import JiraClient, JiraError\n12|from .telemetry_service import TelemetryService\n13|\n14|\n15|# Correlation: Jira key stored on the Work Item title/scope prefix.\n16|JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX = \"jira:\"\n17|\n18|\n19|def correlation_tag(issue_key: str) -> str:\n20|    return f\"{JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX}{issue_key}\"\n21|\n22|\n23|def extract_jira_key(scope_or_title: str) -> str | None:\n24|    idx = scope_or_title.find(JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX)\n25|    if idx < 0:\n26|        return None\n27|    rest = scope_or_title[idx + len(JIRA_CORRELATION_PREFIX):]\n28|    token = rest.split()[0].split(\"\\n\")[0].strip()\n29|    return token or None\n30|\n31|\n32|class JiraSyncService:\n33|    \"\"\"Stateless-ish orchestration over JiraClient; db session injected.\"\"\"\n34|\n35|    def __init__(self, client: JiraClient | None = None) -> None:\n36|        self.client = client or JiraClient()\n37|\n38|    async def inbound_assigned_issues(self, db) -> list[dict]:\n39|        \"\"\"Fetch assigned issues and record an inbound-sync event per issue.\n40|\n41|        Returns issue dicts for the Executive Agent to decompose. Does NOT\n42|        create Work Items automatically (Executive decides; plan §D3/D4).\n43|        \"\"\"\n44|        issues = self.client.search_assigned_issues()\n45|        out = []\n46|        for issue in issues:\n47|            await TelemetryService.record_event(\n48|                db,\n49|                event_type=\"JIRA_INBOUND_SYNCED\",\n50|                summary=f\"Jira {issue.key} assigned: {issue.summary[:120]}\",\n51|                actor_source=\"jira_sync\",\n52|                metadata={\"issue_key\": issue.key, \"status\": issue.status,\n53|                          \"priority\": issue.priority, \"url\": issue.url},\n54|            )\n55|            out.append({\n56|                \"key\": issue.key, \"summary\": issue.summary,\n57|                \"description\": issue.description, \"status\": issue.status,\n58|                \"priority\": issue.priority, \"labels\": issue.labels,\n59|                \"url\": issue.url, \"correlation_tag\": correlation_tag(issue.key),\n60|            })\n61|        return out\n62|\n63|    async def outbound_summary(self, db, *, issue_key: str, kind: str, detail: str) -> str:\n64|        \"\"\"Post one lifecycle summary comment (started/milestone/blocked/pr/completed/handoff).\"\"\"\n65|        body = f\"[ACMS Executive] {kind.upper()}: {detail}\"\n66|        comment_id = self.client.add_comment(issue_key, body)\n67|        await TelemetryService.record_event(\n68|            db,\n69|            event_type=\"JIRA_OUTBOUND_POSTED\",\n70|            summary=f\"Jira {issue_key} <- {kind}: {detail[:120]}\",\n71|            actor_source=\"jira_sync\",\n72|            metadata={\"issue_key\": issue_key, \"kind\": kind, \"comment_id\": comment_id},\n73|        )\n74|        return comment_id\n75|", "total_lines": 74, "file_size": 3080, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|# ADR-0017: Projects/Products/Tags, Jira reconciliation controls, Human Inbox, and Artifacts\n2|\n3|**Status:** Proposed (human direction: STEA-004 execution plan 2026-10-01 §6/§9–§11/§23–§25)\n4|**Date:** 2026-10-01\n5|\n6|## Context\n7|\n8|Work Items have no Project/Product home; Jira status changes don't control\n9|downstream execution; the Attention feed is a derived list without read/unread\n10|or reply actions; handoff Markdown has no durable home.\n11|\n12|## Decision\n13|\n14|### Data model (one migration, 0012)\n15|\n16|- `products` (id, name, slug unique, business_summary, status, default_model_policy JSON, context_notes, timestamps)\n17|- `projects` (id, name, slug unique, product_id FK nullable, repos TEXT, jira_project_key, default_model_policy JSON, timestamps)\n18|- `work_items.project_id` FK → projects (NULL allowed for legacy rows; new items require it — enforced at API layer)\n19|- `tags` (id, name unique) + `tag_links` (tag_id, scope: work_item|project|product, scope_id)\n20|- `artifacts` (id, work_item_id FK, execution_session_id nullable, agent_id, project_id, product_id nullable, jira_issue_key, artifact_type, title, bluf, mime_type, content TEXT, sha256, created_at/by)\n21|- `human_inbox_items` (id, class: ACTION_REQUIRED|FYI|STALE, severity, title, summary, agent_id, work_item_id, execution_session_id, project_id, product_id, jira_issue_key, correlation_id unique-dedupe, read_at, archived_at, created_at, updated_at, metadata_json)\n22|- `execution_events` (per ADR-0014 live stream persistence)\n23|- `jira_reconcile_actions` stays audit-only (existing event log is the ledger;\n24|  no second store).\n25|\n26|### Jira status → execution control (plan §6)\n27|\n28|The reconciliation service (window-5 `jira_reconcile.start_reconciliation_run`)\n29|gains an ACTION MAP applied AFTER linking, BEFORE dispatch eligibility:\n30|\n31|| Jira status | ACMS effect |\n32||---|---|\n33|| IDEA/UNVALIDATED | not dispatchable (existing ELIGIBLE gate already covers) |\n34|| TO START | dispatchable (existing ready status) |\n35|| IN PROGRESS | dispatchable; running work continues |\n36|| BLOCKED | new dispatch refused; if running → `WORK_HOLD_REQUESTED` event + Inbox ACTION_REQUIRED (steer/stop decision is human-safe default: request safe stop via steer message; never force-kill) |\n37|| DEFERRED | same hold behavior; context preserved; no new execution |\n38|| IN REVIEW | no NEW implementation dispatch; completed output preserved |\n39|| COMPLETED | terminal; no new execution |\n40|\n41|- Mapping verdicts persist on `work_items.jira_eligibility` (+reason) exactly\n42|  like the existing gate vocab, so the UI/board render one source.\n43|- ACMS never writes Jira status except the §25 output link/comment under the\n44|  explicit `jira_status_mutation_enabled=false` rule (write-back limited to\n45|  comments + description edit via dedicated helpers; status transitions stay\n46|  human).\n47|\n48|### Products/Projects UI (plan §11)\n49|\n50|Replace `+ New Product/Idea` ambiguity: `/ui/products` gets `+ Create Product`\n51|and `+ Create Project` with one-line definitions; Product view shows related\n52|project chips (removable), work counts by execution state, Jira issues,\n53|context notes; Project view shows product, repos, Jira mapping, active work,\n54|artifacts, inbox items.\n55|\n56|### Human Inbox (plan §23/§24)\n57|\n58|- `/ui/inbox` replaces `/ui/attention` nav label (feed API stays as derivation\n59|  source; Inbox adds durable rows).\n60|- Read/unread/mark-unread; Archive with force-warning for unresolved\n61|  ACTION_REQUIRED; auto-demotion ACTION_REQUIRED → FYI/STALE when the linked\n62|  execution resolves (done in inbox service on completion callback).\n63|- Reply actions: `steer` (bridge steer to the bound session — existing tested\n64|  endpoint), `incorporate` (append durable context to work item scope +\n65|  optional Jira comment), `reply-only` (record), `archive`.\n66|- Context Markdown button: on-demand generation → stored as an Artifact.\n67|- Caveman-style short summaries required on creation (≤120 chars).\n68|- Rate guardrail: related events within a window aggregate into one item\n69|  (correlation_id dedupe); never suppress critical safety items.\n70|\n71|### Artifacts (plan §25)\n72|\n73|- Artifact subsystem: DB-authoritative Markdown (`artifacts.content`), UI at\n74|  `/ui/artifacts/{id}` + API list/get/download.\n75|- Jira output: description `Output Artifacts` gains\n76|  `ACMS Handoff: https://10.0.20.122/ui/artifacts/<id>`; a short BLUF comment\n77|  (BLUF / what changed / result / test evidence / link) — never raw Markdown\n78|  dumps.\n79|- Artifact creation is authenticated: agent callbacks (completion/artifact\n80|  upload) bind to the task's agent; UI/API use session/bearer per existing\n81|  patterns.\n82|\n83|## Consequences\n84|\n85|- All §39 test-matrix items for Jira/Inbox/Artifacts get direct table/service\n86|  coverage; no behavior lives only in templates.\n87|- Legacy work items without `project_id` keep working (nullable), flagged in\n88|  UI as \"unassigned project\".", "total_lines": 87, "file_size": 4802, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

### [2026-10-02T05:27:21.970505+00:00] assistant

## Diagnosis

Repository inspected at:

- `/home/jordatech/work/acms-project-framework`
- `origin/main`
- Commit `7daad53274a3ca96fc1db9c04cc0d43d7b8b6813`
- Working tree remained clean.
- No MCP work, deployment, external Jira access, production-data access, or secret inspection was performed.

### Bottom line

The most likely explanation is deterministic from the code:

> **“Check Jira now” does not create a Work Item for a newly discovered Jira issue.**

For an unlinked `TO START` issue assigned to the configured AI account, reconciliation records an observation and a `JIRA_CANDIDATE_OBSERVED` event, increments `unchanged`, and returns. The run can therefore correctly report `completed` while creating no Work.

Even for an already-linked, planned Work Item, reconciliation only emits `JIRA_WORK_QUEUED` and increments `added`; it does not assign an agent or call the dispatch service. The `started` and `resumed` counters are never incremented anywhere in the reconciliation implementation.

Thus `completed` currently means **the scan completed without a recorded scan problem**, not “every eligible Jira candidate became Work” or “execution started.”

---

# 1. Exact path: manual “Check Jira now” → candidate → Work/dispatch

## 1.1 UI request

1. The Work page renders the POST form:
   - `acms/ui/templates/work_list.html:25-31`
   - Button text says reconciliation may “start, pause, or stop.”

2. POST `/ui/jira/check-now`:
   - `acms/ui/jira_routes.py:27-45`
   - Requires administrator at line 39.
   - Calls `check_jira_now()` at line 41.
   - Redirects with only `jira_run`, `jira_state`, and optional `jira_coalesced` at lines 42-45.

3. API adapter:
   - `acms/jira_api.py:67-76`
   - Calls `start_reconciliation_run(... trigger="manual_check")` at lines 73-74.
   - `ReconcileAccepted` contains only run ID, state, and coalesced flag:
     `acms/jira_api.py:45-49`.

## 1.2 Run creation and execution

4. Lease/coalescing:
   - `acms/jira_reconcile.py:119-161`
   - A live queued/running run is returned without new execution at lines 129-142.
   - New run and `JIRA_RECONCILE_REQUESTED` event are created at lines 144-156.
   - Reconciliation executes synchronously at line 161.

5. Execution begins:
   - `acms/jira_reconcile.py:166-189`
   - Run becomes `running` at lines 170-173.
   - Mock/unconfigured Jira becomes an honest failed run at lines 180-189.

## 1.3 Candidate discovery

6. Candidate search:
   - `acms/jira_reconcile.py:191-209`
   - Calls `JiraClient.search_ai_account_issues()` at line 196.
   - Candidate search results are tagged `candidate_search` at line 197.
   - `examined` increases by the fetched row count at line 198.

7. JQL and assignment identity:
   - `acms/jira_client.py:155-183`
   - The search requires configured immutable `jira_ai_account_id` at lines 162-164.
   - JQL is exact account ID matching:
     `assignee = '<configured-account-id>'` at line 165.
   - Configuration is declared as an immutable Jira account ID:
     `acms/settings.py:72-76`.

   Therefore the visible display name “Startup Teams” does not prove STNA-88 matches the configured account ID. If its immutable account ID differs, it will not enter the candidate list at all.

8. Existing links are separately fetched by immutable Jira issue ID:
   - `acms/jira_reconcile.py:210-223`
   - Only rows in `jira_issue_links` are included.
   - Work-item Jira columns alone are not queried here.

## 1.4 Per-candidate processing

9. Per-candidate handler:
   - `acms/jira_reconcile.py:274-315`
   - Deduplicates by issue ID/key at lines 283-287.
   - Looks up `JiraIssueLinkRecord` by issue ID at lines 289-293.
   - Appends an observation at lines 295-297.

10. **Unlinked branch — the critical STNA-88 path:**
    - `acms/jira_reconcile.py:299-315`
    - Comments explicitly state intake/Work creation is not automatic:
      lines 300-303.
    - If status is configured ready, it:
      - increments `unchanged`, line 305;
      - emits `JIRA_CANDIDATE_OBSERVED`, lines 306-312;
      - returns, line 315.
    - It does **not** call:
      - `work_service.create_work_item`;
      - `gate_work_item`;
      - `assign_primary`;
      - `dispatch_work`;
      - any import endpoint.

11. **Linked branch:**
    - Loads the already-existing Work Item:
      `acms/jira_reconcile.py:317-321`.
    - Compares the observed account ID to the configured AI ID:
      lines 329-330.
    - Applies status mapping at lines 332-377.
    - A ready issue enters `_apply_ready()` at lines 368-369.

12. Ready linked Work behavior:
    - `acms/jira_reconcile.py:388-433`
    - Active Work is simply unchanged, lines 403-405.
    - STOP/PAUSE holds prevent action, lines 406-417.
    - Planned Work:
      - sets `last_generation_started_at = None`, line 424;
      - increments `added`, line 425;
      - emits `JIRA_WORK_QUEUED`, lines 426-431.
    - It does not alter Work status, create an assignment, create an execution task, or invoke dispatch.

## 1.5 Actual Work creation paths are separate

The real Work creation implementation is:

- `work_service.create_work_item()`:
  `acms/work_service.py:48-79`.

It is called from:

- Manual UI creation:
  `acms/ui/work_routes.py:180-205`.
- Machine API:
  `acms/work_api.py:115-138`.
- Product bootstrap hierarchy:
  `acms/product_bootstrap.py:373-408`.

There is no call from `jira_reconcile.py`.

The existing Jira link endpoint also requires a Work Item to already exist:

- `acms/jira_api.py:125-173`
- It loads Work at lines 133-135, then creates `JiraIssueLinkRecord` at lines 142-158.

There is no Jira-candidate import endpoint in the repository.

## 1.6 Actual eligibility and dispatch path is also separate

Execution only starts through:

- API:
  `acms/dispatch_api.py:39-63`.
- Service:
  `acms/dispatch_service.py:146-229`.

Requirements before the Jira gate include:

- Existing Work Item: `dispatch_service.py:160-163`.
- Active assignment or explicitly supplied agent: lines 164-171.
- Worker availability: lines 182-200.
- Live Jira gate: lines 202-229.

The gate itself:

- Resolves own/inherited linkage:
  `acms/jira_gate.py:214-250`.
- Performs live read:
  `acms/jira_gate.py:255-285`.
- Evaluates exact account ID, status, and holds:
  `acms/jira_gate.py:109-163`.
- Persists the verdict on the Work Item:
  `acms/jira_gate.py:353-363`.

Reconciliation never invokes this dispatch path.

# 2. Current outcome instrumentation

## Durable run ledger

`JiraReconciliationRunRecord` persists:

- state/timestamps/lease;
- examined;
- added;
- started;
- resumed;
- paused and acknowledged;
- stopped and acknowledged;
- unchanged;
- failed;
- scan completeness;
- error summary.

Definitions:

- `acms/jira_models.py:99-130`
- Migration:
  `migrations/versions/0010_jira_kickoff_gate.py:81-105`

Population occurs at:

- `acms/jira_reconcile.py:244-258`

Run APIs expose these counts:

- List:
  `acms/jira_api.py:79-96`
- Detail:
  `acms/jira_api.py:99-113`

## Audit events

Current reconciliation events include:

- `JIRA_RECONCILE_REQUESTED`:
  `acms/jira_reconcile.py:150-155`
- `JIRA_RECONCILE_FAILED` for mock/unconfigured integration:
  lines 180-189
- `JIRA_CANDIDATE_OBSERVED`:
  lines 299-312
- `JIRA_WORK_QUEUED`:
  lines 419-431
- `JIRA_WORK_IN_REVIEW`:

  lines 406-413

  lines 436-460

  `acms/dispatch_service.py:182-229`, `231-253`
- `EXECUTION_DISPATCHED`:
  `acms/dispatch_service.py:395-412`

## Observation history

Successful Jira reads become append-only observations:

- `acms/jira_reconcile.py:527-540`

Linked direct-read failures become failed observations:

- `acms/jira_reconcile.py:543-556`

# 3. Instrumentation gaps that make the skip appear silent

1. **UI discards outcome counts.**
   - Redirect carries only state/run/coalesced:
     `acms/ui/jira_routes.py:42-45`.
   - Work page shows only run ID and state:
     `acms/ui/templates/work_list.html:35-41`.

2. **The advertised run-history UI does not exist.**
   - Template links to `/ui/work?jira_history=1` at line 40.
   - `work_list()` ignores `jira_history` and never loads reconciliation runs:
     `acms/ui/work_routes.py:112-137`.
   - Repository search found no other `jira_history` implementation.

3. **Unlinked ready candidates are counted as `unchanged`.**
   - `acms/jira_reconcile.py:299-315`
   - There is no `candidate_observed`, `unlinked`, `import_required`, or `skipped` counter.

4. **Unlinked non-ready candidates get no issue-specific event at all.**
   - Only `unchanged += 1`, lines 313-315.

5. **Several linked no-op branches have no reason-level event.**
   - Active Work: lines 403-405.
   - PAUSE hold on ready Work: lines 415-417.
   - Proven in-progress generation: lines 366-367.
   - Ready Work in another status: lines 432-433.
   - Duplicates return before any outcome counter/event: lines 283-287.

6. **`started` and `resumed` are dead counters in reconciliation.**
   - Defined at `acms/jira_reconcile.py:81-82`.
   - Persisted at lines 250-251.
   - No reconciliation code increments either counter.

7. **`added` is misleading.**
   - It means an already-existing planned Work Item produced a `JIRA_WORK_QUEUED` event.
   - No Work is added and no durable queue state is changed:
     `acms/jira_reconcile.py:419-431`.
   - It can be incremented again on later polls because generation/start state is not advanced.

8. **`completed` does not require useful candidate outcomes.**
   - State is based only on scan completeness and whether `problems` is empty:
     `acms/jira_reconcile.py:244-245`.
   - A run with all candidates unlinked/unchanged is `completed`.

9. **`failed_count > 0` can still produce `completed`.**
   - A link whose Work row is missing increments `failed` and returns:
     `acms/jira_reconcile.py:317-321`.
   - It does not append to `problems`; state can remain completed.

10. **No structured per-issue reconciliation outcome exists.**
    - Observations have no `run_id` column:
      `acms/jira_models.py:46-65`.
    - Successful observation `detail_json` is empty:
      `acms/jira_reconcile.py:527-540`.
    - Run rows contain only aggregate counts.

11. **Event metadata is not exposed by the event API.**
    - Reconciliation events include `run_id`/issue metadata internally.
    - `AgentEventView` and `/events` return summary fields but omit metadata:
      `acms/telemetry_api.py:140-175`.

12. **Reconciliation does not update Work’s persisted Jira eligibility fields.**
    - `_observe()` only inserts an observation:
      `acms/jira_reconcile.py:527-540`.
    - Work-level fields are updated only by `gate_work_item()`:
      `acms/jira_gate.py:353-363`.
    - Reconciliation does not call that gate, so UI eligibility can remain stale.

13. **`examined` is a fetch count, not a unique candidate/outcome count.**
    - It counts candidate-search rows and direct linked fetches independently:
      `acms/jira_reconcile.py:196-198`, `210-217`.
    - The later dedupe at lines 283-287 means examined can exceed processed issues.

14. **Pagination can silently stop after the first page.**
    - Client only advances if the response has a numeric `total`:
      `acms/jira_client.py:179-183`.
    - Any successful response shape without `total` causes `nxt=None`, with no partial/error signal.
    - This is a code robustness issue; repository-only inspection cannot establish whether it affected STNA-88.

---

# 4. Likely root causes, ranked

## Root cause 1 — highest confidence: STNA-88 had no pre-existing Jira link/Work

This exactly matches the code:

- Search finds ready AI-assigned STNA-88.
- `_process_observation()` cannot find a `jira_issue_links` row.
- It emits `JIRA_CANDIDATE_OBSERVED`, increments `unchanged`, and returns.
- Run finishes `completed`.
- No Work is created.

Relevant code: `acms/jira_reconcile.py:289-315`.

## Root cause 2 — split-brain linkage representation

There are two linkage representations:

1. Jira columns on `work_items`;
2. `jira_issue_links`.

Reconciliation candidate correlation uses only `jira_issue_links`:

- `acms/jira_reconcile.py:289-293`
- linked direct scans use the same table at lines 210-223.

But product bootstrap only writes fields on `WorkItemRecord`:

- `acms/product_bootstrap.py:439-449`

It does not create a `JiraIssueLinkRecord`. A bootstrap-linked issue can consequently be treated as unlinked by reconciliation.

The ordinary link endpoint correctly writes both representations:

- link row: `acms/jira_api.py:142-158`
- Work fields: lines 159-163.

Which path created/linked STNA-87 versus STNA-88 is therefore an important comparison point.

## Root cause 3 — “Startup Teams” may not be the configured immutable Jira account

Candidate JQL matches exact account ID, not display name:

- `acms/jira_client.py:155-165`.

If STNA-88’s “Startup Teams” assignee resolves to a different account ID than `ACMS_JIRA_AI_ACCOUNT_ID`, it is absent from the scan. An empty successful result still yields a `completed` run.

This cannot be confirmed without live Jira/config access, which was intentionally not performed.

## Root cause 4 — even a linked candidate is not started by reconciliation

For existing linked planned Work:

- only `added++` and `JIRA_WORK_QUEUED` happen:
  `acms/jira_reconcile.py:419-431`.

There is no assignment selection or dispatch. Therefore the UI claim that Check Jira now “starts eligible work” is not implemented by this path.

## Root cause 5 — pagination/search omission

The client assumes `total`/`startAt` pagination:

- `acms/jira_client.py:174-183`.

If the Jira endpoint returns a successful page without `total`, only the first page is scanned and the run can still be full/completed.

---

# 5. STNA-86 / STNA-87 / STNA-88 comparison

## Repository facts

### STNA-86

- Defined as the canonical ticket template:
  `acms/jira_template.py:1-12`, `22-34`.
- Validator fetches STNA-86 and compares required sections:
  `acms/jira_template.py:66-113`.
- Tests:
  `tests/test_uid_keys.py:291-331`.

There is no repository evidence that STNA-86 exercised automatic Jira candidate intake.

### STNA-87

Repository comments state:

- It was the prior acceptance ticket.
- It was created as a one-time approximation of STNA-86 rather than a faithful clone:
  `acms/jira_template.py:1-5`.
- Jira REST v3 comment-format handling was hit during an STNA-87 correction:
  `acms/jira_client.py:223-230`.

The user-supplied fact that STNA-87 succeeded does not prove the generic reconciliation path created it; it may have been manually created, linked, assigned, and dispatched through the separate Work/dispatch APIs.

### STNA-88

- No STNA-88 content or history reference exists in the repository.
- Therefore repository inspection cannot identify its live issue ID, assignee account ID, link row, observation, or Work row.

## Precise live-state comparison points for 86/87/88

Without printing secrets, operators should compare:

1. Exact Jira `issue.id`, not just key.
2. Exact `assignee.accountId`, not display name.
3. Exact status text after trim/case normalization.
4. Whether each issue appears in `search_ai_account_issues`.
5. Search page position and pagination response shape.
6. `jira_issue_links` row by immutable issue ID.
7. Corresponding `work_items` Jira fields.
8. Whether linkage exists only in Work columns but not `jira_issue_links`.
9. Latest `jira_issue_observations` row and source.
10. `JIRA_CANDIDATE_OBSERVED`, `JIRA_WORK_QUEUED`, and reconcile completion events for the run.
11. Work status, active assignment, local holds, and execution task.
12. Whether STNA-87 was created/link-dispatched manually rather than by reconciliation.

The highest-value comparison is:

> **Does STNA-87 have a `jira_issue_links` row and pre-existing Work while STNA-88 does not?**

# 6. Precise files and tests needing changes

## Production code

### `acms/jira_reconcile.py`

Required changes around:

- candidate discovery: `191-223`;
- per-issue outcome: `274-377`;
- ready action: `388-433`;
- run state/count persistence: `244-269`;
- observations: `527-556`.

Needed behavior:

- Define whether admin-triggered reconciliation imports candidates automatically or produces an explicit durable “awaiting import” item.
- Do not count an unlinked ready candidate as generic `unchanged`.
- Record one structured outcome per unique issue.
- Make `added` mean actual Work creation, or rename it.
- Either invoke a real intake/assignment/dispatch path or remove the “starts work” claim.
- Base run state on issue failures as well as the `problems` list.

### `acms/dispatch_service.py`

Relevant areas:

- prerequisites: `160-200`;
- Jira gate: `202-229`.

Only change this if reconciliation is intended to start execution. The reconciliation/intake path must first establish:

- a Work Item;
- an active assignment or deterministic authorized agent choice;
- instructions;
- idempotency/generation state.

It should still converge on `dispatch_work()` rather than duplicate dispatch logic.
